# Graph Report - vega  (2026-11-21)

## Corpus Check
- 3350 files · ~5,584,778 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 762 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 329, .scm 165, .wit 77)

## Summary
- 113791 nodes · 353584 edges · 2714 communities (1689 shown, 1025 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 13545 edges (avg confidence: 0.76)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5804fb38`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
